CHAPTER-III 
GENERAL 


Repeal and savings.— The National Highways Authority of India (Recruitment, Seniority and Promotion) 
Regulations, 1996 and the National Highways Authority of India, Finance Cadre [For posts of DGM (Finance 
and Accountants), Manager (Finance and Accounts), Deputy Manager (Finance and Accounts), Assistant 
Manager (Finance and Accounts) and Accountant] (Recruitment, Seniority and Promotion) Regulations, 2021 
and any subsequent Regulations, Amendments or orders corresponding to the provisions in these Regulations in 
force immediately before the commencement of these Regulations and applicable to the employees of the 
Authority are hereby repealed: 


Provided that such repeal shall not affect the previous operation of the said Regulations or orders, as 
the case may be, or anything duly done or suffered thereunder or affect any right, liability or obligations 
already acquired or incurred under the said Regulations or orders, as the case may be. 


Provided further that wherever, in these Regulations, the eligibility service for promotion prescribed in 
the existing rules is being enhanced and the change is likely to affect adversely any officer or employee or 
some persons holding the feeder grade post on regular basis, the eligibility service for promotion shall 
continue to be the same for persons holding the feeder posts on regular basis on the date of notification of 
these regulations. 


Residuary matters.— In regard to any matters not specifically covered by the Regulations made under Section 
35 of the Act or by any general or special orders issued by the National Highways Authority of India, the service 
conditions of the employees of Authority shall be governed by the rules or instructions applicable to the 
employees of the Central Government, from time to time. 


Interpretation.— In case of any doubt relating to the interpretation of any provision of these Regulations, the 
decision of the Authority, with reasons recorded in writing, shall be final. If there is a conflict between the 
interpretation of a provision in the Regulation and the Schedule or if these are at variance, the provision under 
the Regulation shall prevail. 


Power to relax.— Where the Authority is satisfied that the operation of any of the above mentioned regulations 
causes undue hardship to any class or category of persons, it may dispense with or relax the requirement of the 
regulation to such extent and subject to such conditions, as it may consider necessary and expedient to do so. 


Saving.— Nothing in these Regulations shall affect reservation, relaxation of age limit and other concessions 
required to be provided for candidates belonging to the Scheduled Castes (SC), the Scheduled Tribes (ST), the 
Other Backward Classes (OBC), the Ex-servicemen, the Persons with Benchmark Disability (PwBD), the 
Economically Weaker Sections (EWS), who are not covered under the scheme of reservation for SCs, STs and 
OBCs and other special categories of persons in accordance with the instructions issued by the Central 
Government, from time to time, in this regard.

or equivalent.

07 years.

should have more than two years of remaining service, as on the closing date of receipt of applications, before 
superannuation in the parent department.

State Governments or Union Territories or Universities or

and 
(ii) At least five years experience in electrical installation and wiring. 


Note-1: The existing Electrician working on contract basis shall be 
allowed to continue on contract basis, based on requirement of the 
organisation. 


Note-2: The period of deputation including the period of deputation 
in another ex-cadre post held immediately preceding _ this 
appointment in the same or some other organisation or department 


[भाग ॥|-खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र : असाधारण 195 


shall ordinarily be 01 to 05 years. The period of deputation upto 05 
years may be decided with the approval of Chairman. The period of 
deputation may further be extended for 06" year with the approval 
of Secretary, Ministry of Road Transport and Highways and for the 
07" year with the approval of Hon’ble Minister for Road Transport 
and Highways, subject to satisfactory performance. 


The maximum age-limit for appointment by deputation shall not be 
exceeding fifty-six years. The candidates should have more than 
two years of remaining service, as on the closing date of receipt of 
applications, before superannuation in the parent department. 


# The post of Electrician will stand abolished after it falls vacant on promotion or superannuation or resignation or 
otherwise of the existing incumbent.
